## Summary
<h2>2026.10.8</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Updates that finish and recover:</strong> an ordinary post-update Doctor failure restarts the Gateway instead of leaving it stopped, an orphaned maintenance worker no longer blocks every CLI command and Gateway start, and updates no longer abort on ACL filesystems, Windows pnpm optional-dependency links, pnpm 10 global installs, debug console logging, or a missing legacy OAuth directory. <li><strong>Gateway service installs on every desktop:</strong> macOS recovers a loaded launchd registration whose plist was deleted, Linux installs under the Ubuntu default umask create a private state directory instead of being refused, and the macOS updater waits for the serving Gateway to release its databases before Doctor runs. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5974522575" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180390" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180390/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180390">#180390</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5961929307" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179538" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179538/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179538">#179538</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6007607243" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182055" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182055/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182055">#182055</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/avnishkirnalli/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/avnishkirnalli">@avnishkirnalli</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/XiaoPengYouCode/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/XiaoPengYouCode">@XiaoPengYouCode</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Conversations keep accepting messages:</strong> killed runs release their restart-recovery fence, interrupted and just-accepted child sessions recover after a Gateway restart, inbound messages stop retrying forever when the reply runtime cannot publish, and the Control UI forgotten-send retry delivers the reply instead of an authoring error. <li><strong>Startup and database resilience:</strong> startup work no longer inherits a closed SQLite reader scope, so models, chat metadata and channel replies stay available; transient lock contention during agent database startup is retried; and model catalogs stay available during skill runs. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6013361786" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182615" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182615/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182615">#182615</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6011137169" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182446">#182446</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6010727644" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182385" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182385/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182385">#182385</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Security hardening:</strong> exec allowlist mode requires approval for <code>test -v</code> operand expansion, generic node invoke refuses browser proxy commands, sandboxed spawned children cannot run host CLI backends, the secret egress proxy rejects distrusted eMudhra chains, Fish Audio and Gradium errors no longer expose API keys, and deeply nested Tlon content can no longer stall the channel. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Fresh OpenAI setup:</strong> first-run model metadata no longer restricts the model picker to one model. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5904575343" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175945" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945">#175945</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/matthematics1137/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/matthematics1137">@matthematics1137</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jb-dev1/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jb-dev1">@jb-dev1</a>.</li>
</ul>

## Why It Matters
Source signal: <h2>2026.10.8</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Updates that finish and recover:</strong> an ordinary post-update Doctor failure restarts the Gateway instead of leaving it stopped, an orphaned maintenance worker no longer blocks every CLI command and Gateway start, and updates no longer abort on ACL filesystems, Windows pnpm optional-dependency links, pnpm 10 global installs, debug console logging, or a missing legacy OAuth directory. <li><strong>Gateway service installs on every desktop:</strong> macOS recovers a loaded launchd registration whose plist was deleted, Linux installs under the Ubuntu default umask create a private state directory instead of being refused, and the macOS updater waits for the serving Gateway to release its databases before Doctor runs. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5974522575" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180390" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180390/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180390">#180390</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5961929307" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/179538" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179538/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/179538">#179538</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6007607243" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182055" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182055/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182055">#182055</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/avnishkirnalli/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/avnishkirnalli">@avnishkirnalli</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/XiaoPengYouCode/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/XiaoPengYouCode">@XiaoPengYouCode</a>.</li>

<li><strong>Startup and database resilience:</strong> startup work no longer inherits a closed SQLite reader scope, so models, chat metadata and channel replies stay available; transient lock contention during agent database startup is retried; and model catalogs stay available during skill runs. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6013361786" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182615" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182615/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182615">#182615</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6011137169" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182446" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182446/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182446">#182446</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6010727644" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182385" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182385/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182385">#182385</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Security hardening:</strong> exec allowlist mode requires approval for <code>test -v</code> operand expansion, generic node invoke refuses browser proxy commands, sandboxed spawned children cannot run host CLI backends, the secret egress proxy rejects distrusted eMudhra chains, Fish Audio and Gradium errors no longer expose API keys, and deeply nested Tlon content can no longer stall the channel. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Fresh OpenAI setup:</strong> first-run model metadata no longer restricts the model picker to one model. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5904575343" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/175945" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175945">#175945</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/matthematics1137/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/matthematics1137">@matthematics1137</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/jb-dev1/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/jb-dev1">@jb-dev1</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Channel accounts:</strong> default Slack, Google Chat, X, Nextcloud Talk and Zalo accounts keep starting and keep their secrets after a named account is added. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5911347434" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/176267" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176267/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176267">#176267</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5975866051" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/180450" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180450/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180450">#180450</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6013277844" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/182608" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182608/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/182608">#182608</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/555aaditya/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/555aaditya">@555aaditya</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/progerjkd/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/progerjkd">@progerjkd</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/masatohoshino/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/masatohoshino">@masatohoshino</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Slack:</strong> an isolated <code>invalid_auth</code> is retried before a socket-mode channel is disabled, and silent relay network drops reconnect. <li><strong>Subagents and agent turns:</strong> model fallback stops after local runtime faults, recovered child results deliver after session reuse, private completion receipts reconcile after a restart, parents keep waiting for active collectors, batched tool calls settle before <code>sessions_yield</code>, delayed <code>sessions_send</code> replies are no longer cut at 4,096 characters, turns finish after tool-result recovery, messages sent during transcript rebuilds are recovered, and completed attempt callbacks are released. <li><strong>Updates, installs and Doctor:</strong> Windows managed updates no longer lock themselves on TEMP aliases, the Windows installer runs through <code>iex</code>, updates complete during channel outages and keep accepted policy pins, stopped database backups allow readers, Doctor restores the Gateway after SIGHUP, keeps valid reseeded configs and inherited logins, repairs unsupported Node runtimes, recovers agents after deletion journal loss, restores plugin host links, handles very large retained histories, and uses less memory on large fleets.
